\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\leq\exp\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{2}{\mu}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t}}\right)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{T}{\alpha}\big(\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{1}\|_{0}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g\|_{0}\big)^{2}}}\right\},

for all
t\in[0,T]
. Thus, from (
5
), it is immediate that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t+\alpha\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t+\frac{\beta}{2^{r-1}}\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

\displaystyle\leq\exp\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{\frac{1}{\mu^{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{02}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{T}{\alpha}\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\|g_{2}\|_{0}^{2}}}\right)}}\right\}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{T}{\alpha}\big(\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{1}\|_{0}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g\|_{0}\big)^{2}}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\mu,\alpha,\beta,\|\boldsymbol{v}_{02}\|_{\mathbb{H}},\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}},\|g_{1}\|_{0},\|g_{2}\|_{0},T\big)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|g\|_{0}^{2}}}\right\},

and (
5.1
) follows.

Case II:

d=2,3
and
r>3
.

An estimate similar to (
3.17
) yields

\displaystyle|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{2},\boldsymbol{v}}}\right)|

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\||\boldsymbol{v}||\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq\frac{\mu}{2}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\mu}\||\boldsymbol{v}||\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(5.11)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu}{2}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{4}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\boldsymbol{v}|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{r-3}{2\mu(r-1)}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{4}{\beta\mu(r-1)}}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r-3}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

for
r>3
. Combining (
5
), (
5.7
) and (
5
), we get

\displaystyle\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{2},\boldsymbol{v}}}\right)+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{2},\boldsymbol{v}}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\beta}{2}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}_{1}|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\boldsymbol{v}|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{4}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\boldsymbol{v}|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}-\frac{r-3}{2\mu(r-1)}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{4}{\beta\mu(r-1)}}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r-3}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}-\frac{\mu}{2}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(5.12)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\beta}{2^{r}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}-\frac{r-3}{2\mu(r-1)}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{4}{\beta\mu(r-1)}}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r-3}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}-\frac{\mu}{2}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Plugging the estimates (
5.5
) and (
5
) in (
5
), and integrating it from
0
to
t
, we deduce

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\frac{\beta}{2^{r-1}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{t}{\alpha}\big(\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{1}\|_{0}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g\|_{0}\big)^{2}

(5.13)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{r-3}{\mu(r-1)}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{4}{\beta\mu(r-1)}}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r-3}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s,

for all
t\in[0,T]
. By the virtue of Gronwall’s inequality in (
5
), followed by taking supremum on both sides
over time from
0
to
T

easily leads to (
5.1
).

Case III:

d=r=3
with
\beta\mu\geq 1
.
From (
5
), we get

(5.14)

\displaystyle\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}|^{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|^{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{2},\boldsymbol{v}}}\right)\geq\frac{\beta}{2}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}_{1}||\boldsymbol{v}|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{2}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}_{2}||\boldsymbol{v}|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

We use the Cauchy-Schwarz and Young’s inequalities to obtain

(5.15)

\displaystyle|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{2},\boldsymbol{v}}}\right)|\leq\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}_{2}||\boldsymbol{v}|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq\frac{\mu}{2}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\mu}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}_{2}||\boldsymbol{v}|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Gathering (
5.14
) and (
5.15
), we have

\displaystyle\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}|^{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|^{2}\boldsymbol{v}_{2},\boldsymbol{v}}}\right)+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{2},\boldsymbol{v}}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\beta}{2}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}_{1}||\boldsymbol{v}|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(\frac{\beta}{2}-\frac{1}{2\mu}\bigg)\big\||\boldsymbol{v}_{2}||\boldsymbol{v}|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}-\frac{\mu}{2}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(5.16)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\beta-\frac{1}{\mu}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{4}-\frac{\mu}{2}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Plugging the estimates (
5.5
) and (
5
) in (
5
), and integrating it from
0
to
t
, we infer

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\beta-\frac{1}{\mu}}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{4}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{t}{\alpha}\big(\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{1}\|_{0}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g\|_{0}\big)^{2},

and (
5.1
) follows, provided
\beta\mu>1
.

Since
\boldsymbol{v}\in\mathrm{C}^{1}([T/8,T];\mathbb{V})
(see Remark
B.8
), by the mean value theorem, there exists a time
t_{3}\in\big(\frac{3T}{8},\frac{4T}{8}\big)
such that

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t_{3})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}=\frac{1}{\frac{4T}{8}-\frac{3T}{8}}\int_{3T/8}^{4T/8}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\leq\frac{8}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t,

which leads to (
5.2
) from (
5.1
) and it completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 5.2
.

Let the assumptions of Lemma
5.1
hold true and let
t_{3}\in\big(\frac{3T}{8},\frac{4T}{8}\big)
be the same time obtained in Lemma
5.1
. For
r\geq 1
, in 2D, and for
r\geq 3
, in 3D, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[t_{3},T]}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\int_{t_{3}}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t+\mu^{2}\int_{t_{3}}^{T}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

(5.17)

\displaystyle\leq C\big\{\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|g\|_{0}^{2}\big\}.

Moreover, there exists a time
t_{4}\in\big(\frac{4T}{8},\frac{5T}{8}\big)
such that

(5.18)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t_{4})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\leq C\big\{\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|g\|_{0}^{2}\big\}

holds and
C
depends on the input data,
\mu,\alpha,\beta,r
and
T
.

Proof.

Taking the inner product with
\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\cdot)-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(\cdot)
in the equation (
5
) and integrating over
\mathbb{T}^{d}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\frac{\/\mathrm{d}\/}{\/\mathrm{d}\/t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu^{2}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{2}\frac{\/\mathrm{d}\/}{\/\mathrm{d}\/t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\big(\boldsymbol{f}g_{1}(t)+\boldsymbol{f}_{2}g(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\big)+\big(\boldsymbol{f}g_{1}(t)+\boldsymbol{f}_{2}g(t),-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\big)

\displaystyle\quad-\big((\boldsymbol{v}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\big)-\big((\boldsymbol{v}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t),-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\big)

\displaystyle\quad-\big((\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\big)-\big((\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}(t),-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\big)

\displaystyle\quad-\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)-|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)}}\right)

(5.19)

\displaystyle\quad-\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)-|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t),-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)}}\right)=:\sum_{j=1}^{8}I_{j},

where we have used the fact
\langle\nabla p,\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\rangle=0
and
\langle\nabla p,\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\rangle=0
. Next, we estimate each
I_{j}
’s
(j=1,\ldots,8)
separately as follows:
Making use of the Cauchy-Schwarz and Young’s inequalities, we estimate
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
as

(5.20)

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\leq 2\big(\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{1}\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}\big)^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

(5.21)

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\big(\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{1}\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}\big)^{2}+\frac{\mu^{2}}{12}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Case I:

d=2
and
r\geq 1
.

We apply Hölder’s, Gagliardo-Nirenberg’s and Young’s inequalities to estimate
I_{3}
and
I_{4}
as

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{3}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{6}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(5.22)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{4}{3}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{3}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{6}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\mu\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(5.23)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}}{12}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{4}{3}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Similarly, we estimate
I_{5}
and
I_{6}
as

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}}}\right)

(5.24)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\mu^{2}}{12}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\mu\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}}}\right)\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}}{12}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}}}\right)

(5.25)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}}{6}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Using Taylor’s formula (Theorem 7.9.1,
[
8
]
), Agmon’s and Young’s inequalities, we obtain (cf.
[
36
]
)

\displaystyle I_{7}

\displaystyle\leq\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathcal{C}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1})-\mathcal{C}(\boldsymbol{v}_{2})}}\right\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq\beta\bigg\|\int_{0}^{1}\mathcal{C}^{\prime}\big(\theta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}+(1-\theta)\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\big)(\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2})\/\mathrm{d}\/\theta\bigg\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sup_{0<\theta<1}\big\|(\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2})|\theta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}+(1-\theta)\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|^{r-1}

\displaystyle\quad+(r-1)\big(\theta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}+(1-\theta)\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\big)\big|\theta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}+(1-\theta)\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\big|^{r-3}

\displaystyle\quad\times\big((\theta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}+(1-\theta)\boldsymbol{v}_{2})\cdot(\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2})\big)\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}}}\right)^{r-1}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)^{r-1}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(5.26)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)^{2(r-1)}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Using the similar arguments as above, it can be easily deduced that

\displaystyle I_{8}

\displaystyle\leq\beta\mu\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathcal{C}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1})-\mathcal{C}(\boldsymbol{v}_{2})}}\right\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sup_{0<\theta<1}\big\||\theta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}+(1-\theta)\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|^{r-1}\big\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(5.27)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}}{12}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)^{2(r-1)}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Substituting the estimates (
5.20
)-(
5
) in (
5
) and integrating the resulting estimate from
t_{3}
to
t
, we find

\displaystyle\int_{t_{3}}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+2\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu^{2}\int_{t_{3}}^{t}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

\displaystyle\leq 2\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t_{3})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}(t_{3})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+20(t-t_{3})\big(\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\|g_{1}\|_{0}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+C(t-t_{3})^{\frac{2}{3}}\sup_{t\in[t_{3},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{4}{3}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}}}\right)\bigg(\int_{t_{3}}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s\bigg)^{\frac{1}{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+C(t-t_{3})\sup_{t\in[t_{3},T]}\bigg(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\bigg)\sup_{t\in[t_{3},T]}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\sup_{t\in[t_{3},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)^{2(r-1)}

(5.28)

\displaystyle\quad\times(t-t_{3})\sup_{t\in[t_{3},T]}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

for all
t\in[t_{3},T]
. Using the estimates obtained in Lemmas
3.2
,
B.5
and
5.1
, from (
5
), we immediately obtain (
5.2
).

Case II:

d=3
and
r\geq 3
.
We estimate
I_{3}
and
I_{4}

using Hölder’s, Gagliardo-Nirenberg’s and Young’s inequalities as

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{3}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{6}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(5.29)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{3}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{6}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\mu\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(5.30)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}}{12}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Similarly, we compute

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{8}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}}^{\frac{7}{8}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{7}{4}}+\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{7}{4}}}}\right)

(5.31)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\mu^{2}}{12}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{12}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\mu\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{8}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{7}{8}}+\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{7}{8}}}}\right)\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(5.32)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}}{6}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{12}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Using the similar arguments as (
5
), we deduce

\displaystyle I_{7}

\displaystyle\leq\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathcal{C}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1})-\mathcal{C}(\boldsymbol{v}_{2})}}\right\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}}}\right)^{r-1}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}}}\right)^{r-1}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(5.33)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}}}\right)^{2(r-1)}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

and similarly, we obtain

\displaystyle I_{8}

\displaystyle\leq\beta\mu\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathcal{C}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1})-\mathcal{C}(\boldsymbol{v}_{2})}}\right\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sup_{0<\theta<1}\big\||\theta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}+(1-\theta)\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|^{r-1}\big\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(5.34)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}}{12}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}}}\right)^{2(r-1)}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Substituting the estimates (
5.20
), (
5.21
) and (
5.29
)-(
5
) in (
5
) and integrating it from
t_{3}
to
t
, we find

\displaystyle\int_{t_{3}}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+2\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu^{2}\int_{t_{3}}^{t}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

\displaystyle\leq 2\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t_{3})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}(t_{3})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+20(t-t_{3})\big(\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\|g_{1}\|_{0}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+C(t-t)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{t\in[t_{3},T]}\big(\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big)\bigg(\int_{t_{3}}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C(t-t_{3})\sup_{t\in[t_{3},T]}\bigg(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{12}\bigg)\sup_{t\in[t_{3},T]}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\sup_{t\in[t_{3},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|u_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}}}\right)^{2(r-1)}

(5.35)

\displaystyle\quad\times(t-t_{3})\sup_{t\in[t_{3},T]}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

for all
t\in[t_{3},T]
. Thus, from (
5
), we immediately have (
5.2
) by using the estimates obtained in Lemmas
3.2
,
B.5
and
5.1
.

Recalling that
\boldsymbol{v}\in\mathrm{C}^{1}([T/8,T],\mathbb{V})
, there exists a time
t_{4}\in\big(\frac{4T}{8},\frac{5T}{8}\big)
such that

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t_{4})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}=\frac{1}{\frac{5T}{8}-\frac{4T}{8}}\int_{4T/8}^{5T/8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\leq\frac{8}{T}\int_{t_{3}}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t,

which implies (
5.18
) by (
5.2
) and completes the proof.
∎

The following lemma proves the stability of the vector-valued function
\boldsymbol{f}
of the solution of the inverse problem.

Lemma 5.3
.

Let
\boldsymbol{v}_{0i}\in\mathbb{H},\ \boldsymbol{\varphi}_{i}\in\mathbb{H}^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d})\cap\mathbb{V}
,
\nabla\psi_{i}\in\mathbb{G}(\mathbb{T}^{d})
,
g_{i},(g_{i})_{t}\in\mathrm{C}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{T}^{d}\times[0,T]}}\right)
satisfy the assumption (
1.8
), and
\boldsymbol{f}_{i}\in\mathbb{H}
, for
i=1,2
. For
r\geq 1
, in 2D, and for
r\geq 3
, in 3D, we have

(5.36)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\big(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|g\|_{0}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}+\|\nabla\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\nabla\psi-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\big),

where
C
depends on the input data,
\mu,\alpha,\beta,r
and
T
.

Proof.

Differentiating (
5
) with respect to time
t
and then multiplying by
\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\cdot)
to the resulting equation, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\/\mathrm{d}\/}{\/\mathrm{d}\/t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle=((\boldsymbol{f}g_{1t}(t)+\boldsymbol{f}_{2}g_{t}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t))-((\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t))

\displaystyle\quad-((\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t))-((\boldsymbol{v}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t))

(5.37)

\displaystyle\quad-\beta\big(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1})\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}(t)-\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{2})\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\big)=:\sum_{j=1}^{5}E_{j}.

Next, we estimate each
E_{j}
’s
(j=1,\ldots,5)
separately as follows:
Using Hölder’s and Young’s inequalities, we get

(5.38)

\displaystyle E_{1}\leq\frac{1}{2\alpha}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{1t}\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{t}\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}}}\right)^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{2}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Case I:

d=2
and
r\geq 1
.

Making use of Hölder’s, Ladyzhenskaya’s and Young’s inequalities, we obtain the following estimate:

\displaystyle E_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\leq\sqrt{2}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

(5.39)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu}{6}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}},

(5.40)

\displaystyle E_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{2}\leq\sqrt{2}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq\frac{\mu}{6}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{3}{\mu}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

(5.41)

\displaystyle E_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\leq\frac{\mu}{6}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}.

Applying Taylor’s formula and Hölder’s, Agmon’s and Young’s inequalities, one can deduce

\displaystyle E_{5}

\displaystyle=-\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1})\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{2})\boldsymbol{v}_{2t},\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}}}\right)

\displaystyle=-\beta\big\{(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1})(\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}),\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t})+\big((\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1})-\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{2}))\boldsymbol{v}_{2t},\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\big)\big\}

\displaystyle\leq-\beta\big((\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1})-\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{2}))\boldsymbol{v}_{2t},\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\beta\sup_{0<\theta<1}\|\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime}(\theta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}+(1-\theta)\boldsymbol{v}_{2})(\boldsymbol{v}\otimes\boldsymbol{v}_{2t})\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}}}\right)^{r-2}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)^{r-2}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)^{2(r-2)}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(5.42)

\displaystyle\quad+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

for
r\geq 3
. Note that for the case
r=2
, (
5
) holds. For
r=2
, using Hölder’s and Young’s inequalities, we estimate
E_{5}
as

\displaystyle E_{5}

\displaystyle=-\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1})\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{2})\boldsymbol{v}_{2t},\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq-\beta\big((\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1})-\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{2}))\boldsymbol{v}_{2t},\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\big)

\displaystyle\leq-\beta\langle(|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}|-|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|)\boldsymbol{v}_{2t},\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\rangle-\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\langle\frac{\boldsymbol{v}_{1}}{|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}|}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\cdot\boldsymbol{v}_{2t})-\frac{\boldsymbol{v}_{2}}{|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|}(\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\cdot\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(5.43)

\displaystyle\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}.

Plugging the estimates (
5.38
)-(
5
) in (
5
), and integrating the resulting estimate from
t_{4}
to
t
, we find

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{t_{4}}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\alpha\int_{t_{4}}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t_{4})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{(t-t_{4})}{\alpha}\big(\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{1t}\|_{0}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{t}\|_{0}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\int_{t_{4}}^{t}\big(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\big)\|\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{6}{\mu}\int_{t_{4}}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+C\int_{t_{4}}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

\displaystyle\quad+C\int_{t_{4}}^{t}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)^{2(r-2)}

\displaystyle\quad\times\|\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t_{4})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{t-t_{4}}{\alpha}\big(\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{1t}\|_{0}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{t}\|_{0}\big)^{2}+C\sup_{t\in[t_{4},T]}\big(\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\big)

\displaystyle\quad\times(t-t_{4})\sup_{t\in[t_{4},T]}\big(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+C\sup_{t\in[t_{4},T]}\big(\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\big)\int_{t_{4}}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

\displaystyle\quad+C\sup_{t\in[t_{4},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)^{2(r-2)}

(5.44)

\displaystyle\quad\times(t-t_{4})\sup_{t\in[t_{4},T]}\big(\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\big),

for all
t\in[t_{4},T]
. Using the energy estimates obtained in Lemmas
B.5
,
B.6
,
3.2
and
5.2
in the inequality (
5
), it can be easily seen that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[t_{4},T]}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}}}\right),

and as a result, we get

(5.45)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(T)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\big(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|g\|_{0}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}\big).

Using the final overdetermination data in (
5
), one can easily deduce

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{f}g_{1}+\boldsymbol{f}_{2}g

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(T)+(\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{\varphi}+(\boldsymbol{\varphi}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{2}-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}+\nabla\psi

\displaystyle\quad+\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}-|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{2}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{2}}}\right),

which leads to

\displaystyle g_{T}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq\|\boldsymbol{f}g_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g\|_{0}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(T)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{3}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{6}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla\psi-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}+C(\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}+\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}})^{r-1}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g\|_{0}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(T)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)\|\nabla\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\nabla\psi-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)^{r-1}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(5.46)

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(T)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+C\big(\|\nabla\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\nabla\psi-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}+\|g\|_{0}\big),

where we have used Sobolev’s inequality in the above relation. Plugging the relation (
5.45
) in (
5
), we get

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|g\|_{0}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}+\|\nabla\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\nabla\psi-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}}}\right),

which is (
5.36
). For
r=1
, a calculation similar to (
5
) and the use of the same arguments as above lead to the required result.

Case II:

d=3
and
r\geq 3
.
Using Hölder’s, Gagliardo-Nirenberg’s and Young’s inequalities, we obtain the following estimate:

\displaystyle E_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}

(5.47)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu}{6}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}},

(5.48)

\displaystyle E_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{2}\leq C\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\leq\frac{\mu}{6}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

(5.49)

\displaystyle E_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\leq\frac{\mu}{6}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}.

A calculation similar to (
5
) gives

\displaystyle E_{5}

\displaystyle=-\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{1})\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v}_{2})\boldsymbol{v}_{2t},\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}}}\right)^{r-2}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}}}\right)^{r-2}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}}}\right)^{2(r-2)}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

(5.50)

\displaystyle\quad+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

for
r\geq 3
.
Plugging the estimates (
5.38
), (
5.47
)-(
5
) in (
5
), and integrating it from
t_{4}
to
t
, we find

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{t_{4}}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{V}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\alpha\int_{t_{4}}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t_{4})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{t-t_{4}}{\alpha}\big(\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{1t}\|_{0}+\|\boldsymbol{f}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|g_{t}\|_{0}\big)^{2}+C\sup_{t\in[t_{4},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times(t-t_{4})\sup_{t\in[t_{4},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{1t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\sup_{t\in[t_{4},T]}\big(\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{2t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\big)\int_{t_{4}}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

\displaystyle\quad+C\sup_{t\in[t_{4},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}}}\right)^{2(r-2)}

(5.51)

\displaystyle\quad\times(t-t_{4})\sup_{t\in[t_{4},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}}}\right),

for all
t\in[t_{4},T]
.
Using the energy estimates obtained in Lemmas
B.5
-
B.6
,
3.2
and
5.2
in the above inequality, one can easily deduce

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[t_{4},T]}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}}}\right),

and thus, we obtain

(5.52)

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(T)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\big(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|g\|_{0}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}\big).

By using the similar arguments as
d=2
and
r\geq 1
, we finally obtain the relation (
5.36
) (cf. (
5
)).
∎

Proof of part (ii) of Theorem
1.1
.

Stability of the pressure gradient
\nabla p
:
The equation (
5
) can be used to establish the stability of the pressure gradient
\nabla p
.
Taking divergence on both the sides of (
5
) and making the use of divergence free condition on
\boldsymbol{f}_{i}
for
i=1,2
, we get

(5.53)

\displaystyle p

\displaystyle=(-\Delta)^{-1}\big[\nabla\cdot\big\{(\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}+(\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{2}+\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{2}}}\right)\big\}\big],

in the weak sense.

Case II:

d=2
and
r\geq 1
.

Taking gradient on both sides in the equation (
5.53
) and then applying Hölder’s inequality and Taylor’s formula, we deduce

\displaystyle\|\nabla p\|_{\mathbb{H}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|(\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{V}^{\prime}}+\|(\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{V}^{\prime}}+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\||\boldsymbol{v}_{1}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{2}}}\right\|_{\mathbb{V}^{\prime}}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\otimes\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\otimes\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\||\boldsymbol{v}_{1}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{2}}}\right\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r-1}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r-1}}}\right),

where we have used the embedding
\mathbb{V}\subset\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}\subset\mathbb{H}\equiv\mathbb{H}^{\prime}\subset\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\subset\mathbb{V}^{\prime}
. Taking the
(\frac{r+1}{r})^{\text{th}}
power on both sides in the above relation and then integrating it from
0
to
T
, followed by using Hölder’s inequality, we arrive at

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla p(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{-1}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\/\mathrm{d}\/t+C\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

\displaystyle\quad+C\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{\frac{(r-1)(r+1)}{r}}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

\displaystyle\quad+C\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{\frac{(r-1)(r+1)}{r}}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

\displaystyle\leq CT^{\frac{r-1}{2r}}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{4}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{r+1}{4r}}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{4}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{r+1}{4r}}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{4}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{r+1}{4r}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+C\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{1}{r}}\bigg[\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{r-1}{r}}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{r-1}{r}}\bigg].

Using the energy estimates obtained in Lemmas
B.1
and
5.1
in the above inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla p(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}^{-1}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{r}{r+1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|g\|_{0}+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}}}\right)+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}+\|g\|_{0}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}+\|g\|_{0}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}+\|\nabla\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}+\|\nabla\psi-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}}}\right),

where
C
depends on the input data,
\mu,\alpha,\beta,r
and
T
, and we have used Lemma
5.3
.

Case II:

d=3
and
r\geq 3
.

The stability of the pressure gradient can be justified using Taylor’s formula, Hölder’s and interpolation inequalities in (
5.53
), and then energy estimates obtained in the Lemmas
B.1
,
5.1
and
5.3
as follows: For all
\Psi\in\mathrm{L}^{2}(0,T;\mathbb{V})\cap\mathrm{L}^{r+1}(0,T;\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1})

\displaystyle\bigg|\int_{0}^{T}\langle\nabla p(t),\Psi(t)\rangle\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\int_{0}^{T}\langle(\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}(t),\Psi(t)\rangle\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg|+\bigg|\int_{0}^{T}\langle(\boldsymbol{v}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t),\Psi(t)\rangle\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\int_{0}^{T}\big\langle(|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)-|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)),\Psi(t)\big\rangle\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{T}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\frac{2(r+1)}{r-1}}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\frac{2(r+1)}{r-1}}}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}\|\nabla\Psi(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

\displaystyle\quad+C\int_{0}^{T}\|(\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2})(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}\bigg(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}\bigg)^{r-1}\|\Psi(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{T}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{r-3}{r-1}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{\frac{2}{r-1}}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{r-3}{r-1}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{\frac{2}{r-1}}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}\|\nabla\Psi(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

\displaystyle\quad+C\int_{0}^{T}\|(\boldsymbol{v}_{1}-\boldsymbol{v}_{2})(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r-1}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r-1}}}\right)\|\Psi(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{r-3}{2(r-1)}}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\big(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{2}{r^{2}-1}}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\big)\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{r-3}{2(r-1)}}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\big(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{2}{r^{2}-1}}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\big)\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\Psi(t)\|_{\mathbb{V}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\big(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{1}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{2}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\big)\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{r-1}{r+1}}\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\big)\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\Psi(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg(\mu,\alpha,\beta,r,T,\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0i}\|_{\mathbb{H}},\|\boldsymbol{f}_{i}\|_{\mathbb{H}},\|g_{i}\|_{0},T\bigg)\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\big)\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\bigg\{\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\Psi(t)\|_{\mathbb{V}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\bigg(\int_{0}^{T}\|\Psi(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\/\mathrm{d}\/t\bigg)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}\bigg\},

for
i=1,2
. Thus, it is immediate that

\nabla p\in\mathrm{L}^{2}(0,T;\mathbb{H}^{-1})+\mathrm{L}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\big(0,T;{\mathbb{L}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\big)\subset\mathrm{L}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\big(0,T;\mathbb{H}^{-1}+{\mathbb{L}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\big).

and

\displaystyle\|\nabla p\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\big(0,T;\mathbb{H}^{-1}+{\mathbb{L}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\big)}\leq C\|\nabla(p_{1}-p_{2})\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(0,T;\mathbb{H}^{-1})+\mathrm{L}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\big(0,T;{\mathbb{L}}^{\frac{r+1}{r}}\big)}

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{01}-\boldsymbol{v}_{02}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}+\|g_{1}-g_{2}\|_{0}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}+\|(g_{1}-g_{2})_{t}\|_{0}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left.+\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{2})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}+\|\nabla(\psi_{1}-\psi_{2})-\mu\Delta(\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{2})\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}^{\frac{2}{r+1}}}}\right).

From the stability estimate of the pressure gradient
\nabla p
and Lemmas
5.1
-
5.3
, we can see that the solution depends continuously on the data. The Hölder type stability of the solution
\{\boldsymbol{v},\nabla p,\boldsymbol{f}\}
given in (

(
1
)

) and (

(
2
)

) can be established in a similar way.
∎

Appendix A
Proof of Theorem
2.1

Proof of Theorem
2.1
.

Let us assume that the nonlinear operator equation (
2.7
) has a solution within the domain
\mathcal{D}
, denoted as
\boldsymbol{f}
. By substituting
\boldsymbol{f}
into equation (
1.1
), we employ the system (
1.1
)-(
1.4
) to find a pair of functions
(\boldsymbol{v},\nabla p)
as the solution to the direct problem corresponding to the divergence-free external forcing function
\boldsymbol{F}(x,t)=\boldsymbol{f}(x)g(x,t)
.

To establish that
\boldsymbol{v}
and
\nabla p
satisfy the overdetermination condition (
1.5
), we consider

\boldsymbol{v}(x,T)=\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}(x)\ \ \ \ \text{and}\ \ \ \ \nabla p(x,T)=\nabla\psi_{1}(x),\ \ \ \ x\ \in\ \Omega,

and

(A.1)

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(x,T)-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}+(\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}+\alpha\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}+\beta|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}+\nabla\psi_{1}=\boldsymbol{f}g(x,T).

On the other hand, (
2.7
) implies that

(A.2)

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}\boldsymbol{f}-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}+(\boldsymbol{\varphi}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{\varphi}+\alpha\boldsymbol{\varphi}+\beta|\boldsymbol{\varphi}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{\varphi}+\nabla\psi=\boldsymbol{f}g(x,T).

By employing (
2.6
) in (
A.2
) and then combining (
A.1
) and (
A.2
), we can readily confirm that the functions
(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})
and
\nabla(\psi-\psi_{1})
satisfy the following system of equations:

(A.3)

\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{\begin{aligned} -\mu\Delta(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})+\alpha(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})+((\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{\varphi}+((\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}&\cdot\nabla)(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\\
+\beta(|\boldsymbol{\varphi}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{\varphi}-|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})+\nabla(\psi-\psi_{1})&=\boldsymbol{0},\ \ \text{in}\ \Omega,\\
\nabla\cdot(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})&=0,\ \ \text{in}\ \Omega,\\
\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}&=\boldsymbol{0},\ \ \text{on}\ \partial\Omega.\end{aligned}}}\right.

Multiplying with
(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})
to the first equation in (
A.3
), we get

\displaystyle\mu\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle=-(((\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{\varphi},\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})

(A.4)

\displaystyle\quad-\beta(|\boldsymbol{\varphi}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{\varphi}-|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1},\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}).

For
r\geq 1
, we have (see Section 2.4
[
35
,
36
]
)

(A.5)

\displaystyle\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(|\boldsymbol{\varphi}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{\varphi}-|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1},\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\beta}{2}\||\boldsymbol{\varphi}|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{2}\||\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(A.6)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\beta}{2}\||\boldsymbol{\varphi}|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\geq 0.

Case I:

d=2
and
r\in[1,3]
.

Applying Hölder’s, Ladyzhenskaya’s and Young’s inequalities, we obtain

\displaystyle\big|\big(((\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{\varphi},\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq(2)^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}

(A.7)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha}{2}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{3}{4\alpha^{\frac{1}{3}}}\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{\frac{4}{3}}.

Substituting (
A.6
) and (
A
) in (
A
), we obtain

(A.8)

\displaystyle\bigg(\mu-\frac{3}{4\alpha^{\frac{1}{3}}}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{\frac{4}{3}}\bigg)\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{2}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\leq 0.

If
\frac{3}{4\alpha^{\frac{1}{3}}}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{\frac{4}{3}}\leq\mu
and
\alpha>0
, then
\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}=0
and
\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}=0
, so that
\boldsymbol{\varphi}=\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}
and
\nabla\psi=\nabla\psi_{1}
, by using (
A.3
).

Case II:

d=2,3
and
r>3
.

A calculation similar to (
3.17
) gives

\displaystyle\big|\big(((\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\cdot\nabla)(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}),\boldsymbol{\varphi}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu}{2}\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{2}\||\boldsymbol{\varphi}|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(A.9)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{r-3}{2\mu(r-1)}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{2}{\beta\mu(r-1)}}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r-3}}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

for
r>3
.
Substituting (
A.6
) and (
A
) in (
A
), we deduce

\displaystyle\frac{\mu}{2}\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(\alpha-\frac{r-3}{2\mu(r-1)}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{2}{\beta\mu(r-1)}}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r-3}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\leq 0.

If
\frac{r-3}{2\mu(r-1)}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{2}{\beta\mu(r-1)}}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r-3}}<\alpha
and
\mu>0
, then
\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}=0
, so that

\boldsymbol{\varphi}=\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}
and
\nabla\psi=\nabla\psi_{1}
.

Case III:

d=r=3
.
From (
A.6
), we obtain

(A.10)

\displaystyle\beta(|\boldsymbol{\varphi}|^{2}\boldsymbol{\varphi}-|\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}|^{2}\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1},\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\geq\frac{\beta}{2}\||\boldsymbol{\varphi}|(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

\displaystyle\text{and}\ \ \ \ \big|\big(((\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\cdot\nabla)(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}),\boldsymbol{\varphi}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(A.11)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\beta}\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{2}\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Gathering the estimates (
A.10
) and (
A
), and then substituting it in (
A
), we get

(A.12)

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mu-\frac{1}{2\beta}}}\right)\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\leq 0.

For
2\beta\mu\geq 1
and
\alpha>0
, we get
\|\nabla(\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1})\|_{\mathbb{H}}=0
and
\|\boldsymbol{\varphi}-\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}\|_{\mathbb{H}}=0
, and hence, we find

\boldsymbol{\varphi}=\boldsymbol{\varphi}_{1}
and
\nabla\psi=\nabla\psi_{1}
.

On the other hand, let us suppose that the inverse problem (
1.1
)-(
1.5
) admits a solution, denoted as
\{\boldsymbol{v},\nabla p,\boldsymbol{f}\}
. Utilizing the elliptic regularity of the solution, such as the Cattabriga regularity theorem (see
[
7
,
48
]
, etc. or Corollary 2,
[
27
]
) (see Appendix
B
also for regularity results on the solution), when we evaluate the system (
1.1
) at
t=T
, we obtain

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(x,T)-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(x,T)+(\boldsymbol{v}(x,T)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}(x,T)+\alpha\boldsymbol{v}(x,T)

(A.13)

\displaystyle\quad+\beta|\boldsymbol{v}(x,T)|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}(x,T)+\nabla p(x,T)=\boldsymbol{f}(x)g(x,T).

From the given final overdetermination data (
1.5
) and the definition of the operator
\mathcal{A}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}\boldsymbol{f}

\displaystyle-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}+(\boldsymbol{\varphi}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{\varphi}+\alpha\boldsymbol{\varphi}+\beta|\boldsymbol{\varphi}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{\varphi}+\nabla\psi=\boldsymbol{f}g(x,T),

and hence

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{f}=\frac{1}{g(x,T)}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathcal{A}\boldsymbol{f}-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{\varphi}+(\boldsymbol{\varphi}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{\varphi}+\alpha\boldsymbol{\varphi}+\beta|\boldsymbol{\varphi}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{\varphi}+\nabla\psi}}\right)=\mathcal{B}\boldsymbol{f}.

Therefore, we can conclude that the nonlinear operator equation (
2.7
) possesses a solution, thereby completing the proof.
∎

Appendix B
A-priori Energy Estimates

In this section, we obtain a number of
a-priori
estimates for the solutions of the system (
1.1
)-(
1.4
), since the existence, uniqueness and regularity of generalized solutions for the system (
1.1
)-(
1.4
) are known (cf.
[
19
,
27
,
34
,
35
]
, etc.). These estimates are necessary to prove the existence, uniqueness, and stability of the solution of our inverse problem (proof of Theorem
1.1
, see sections
4
and
5
above). To obtain the energy estimates for the solutions of the CBF equations (
1.1
)-(
1.4
), we assume that
\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\in\mathbb{H},

g,g_{t}\in\mathrm{C}(\mathbb{T}^{d}\times[0,T])
satisfy assumption (
1.8
) and
\boldsymbol{f}\in\mathbb{H}
.

Lemma B.1
.

Let
(\boldsymbol{v}(\cdot),\nabla p(\cdot))
be the unique solution of the CBF equations (
1.1
)-(
1.4
) and
\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\in\mathbb{H}
. Then, for
r\geq 1
, the following estimate holds for all
t\in[0,T]
:

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+2\mu\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+2\beta\int_{0}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

(B.1)

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{t}{\alpha}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Proof.

Taking the inner product with
\boldsymbol{v}(\cdot)
in (
1.1
) and integrating the resulting equation over
\mathbb{T}^{d}
, we obtain

(B.2)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\/\mathrm{d}\/}{\/\mathrm{d}\/t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\beta\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}=(\boldsymbol{f}g(t),\boldsymbol{v}(t)),

for a.e.
t\in[0,T]
. Using Hölder’s and Young’s inequalities, we have

(B.3)

\displaystyle|(\boldsymbol{f}g,\boldsymbol{v})|\leq\|g\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq\frac{\alpha}{2}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\alpha}\|g\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Substituting (
B.3
) in (
B.2
) and integrating it from
0
to
t
, we deduce the estimate (
B.1
).
∎

Lemma B.2
.

Let
(\boldsymbol{v}(\cdot),\nabla p(\cdot))
be the unique solution of the CBF equations (
1.1
)-(
1.4
) and
\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\in\mathbb{H}
. Then, for all
t\in[\epsilon,T]
and for any
0<\epsilon<T
,

(i)

for
d=2
and
r\in[1,3],
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[\epsilon,t]}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+2\beta\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(s)|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

(B.4)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\mu}\bigg\{\frac{1}{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(\frac{1}{\alpha}+t\bigg)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\bigg\},

(ii)

for
d=2,3
and
r>3,
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[\epsilon,t]}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\beta\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(s)|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

(B.5)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{1}{2\mu t}+\frac{\eta}{2\mu}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(\frac{1}{2\mu\alpha}+\frac{t}{\mu}+\frac{\eta t}{2\mu\alpha}\bigg)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

(iii)

for
d=r=3
with
\beta\mu>1,
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[\epsilon,t]}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+2\bigg(\beta-\frac{1}{\mu}\bigg)\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(s)||\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

(B.6)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\mu}\bigg\{\frac{1}{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(\frac{1}{\alpha}+2t\bigg)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\bigg\}.

Proof.

Taking the inner product with
-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(\cdot)
in (
1.1
) and integrating it over
\mathbb{T}^{d}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\/\mathrm{d}\/}{\/\mathrm{d}\/t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\beta\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(t)|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(B.7)

\displaystyle=(\boldsymbol{f}g(t),-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t))-((\boldsymbol{v}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}(t),-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)),

for a.e.
t\in[\epsilon,T],
for some
0<\epsilon<T
, where we have used the fact that

\langle\nabla p,-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\rangle=0
(see Lemma
3.2
).

Case I:

d=2
and
r\in[1,3]
.

Substituting the estimates (
3.12
) and (
3.13
) in (
B
), and integrating the resulting estimate from
\epsilon>0
to
t
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+2\beta\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(s)|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

(B.8)

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{t-\epsilon}{\mu}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

for all
t\in[\epsilon,T]
. From the above relation, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\leq\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{t-\epsilon}{\mu}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Integrating the above estimate over
\epsilon
from
0
to
t
, we deduce for all
t\in[\epsilon,T]

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{t}\bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/\epsilon+\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{t-\epsilon}{\mu}\/\mathrm{d}\/\epsilon\bigg),

which leads to (

(
i
)

) from (
B
) by using (
B.1
).

Case II:

d=3
and
r>3
.

Substituting the estimates (
3.15
) and (
3.17
) in (
B
), and integrating it from
\epsilon
to
t
, we have the following estimate:

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\beta\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(s)|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

(B.9)

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{2(t-\epsilon)}{\mu}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\eta\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s,

for all
t\in[\epsilon,T]
, where
\eta=\frac{2(r-3)}{\mu(r-1)}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{4}{\beta\mu(r-1)}}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r-3}}
. Using the estimate (
B.1
) in (
B
), we get

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\leq\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{2(t-\epsilon)}{\mu}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{2\mu}\bigg(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{t}{\alpha}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\bigg),

for all
t\in[\epsilon,T]
. Integrating the above estimate over
\epsilon
from
0
to
t
and then using (
B.1
) in it, we find

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{t}\bigg\{\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/\epsilon+\frac{t^{2}}{\mu}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\eta t}{2\mu}\bigg(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{t}{\alpha}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\bigg)\bigg\},

and, from (
B
), one can arrive at (

(

⁢
i
i

)

) by using (
B.1
).

Case III:

d=r=3
with
\beta\mu>1
.
The term
\beta\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(t)|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}
in (
B
) becomes
\beta\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(t)||\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}
. Substituting the estimates (
3.15
)-(
3.16
) in (
B
), and integrating it from
\epsilon
to
t
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+2\bigg(\beta-\frac{1}{\mu}\bigg)\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(s)||\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

(B.10)

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{2(t-\epsilon)}{\mu}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

for all
t\in[\epsilon,T]
. A calculation similar to
d=2
and
r\in[1,3]
yields the estimate (

(

⁢
i
i
i

)

), provided
\beta\mu>1
.
∎

Lemma B.3
.

Let
(\boldsymbol{v}(\cdot),\nabla p(\cdot))
be the unique solution of the CBF equations (
1.1
)-(
1.4
) and
\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\in\mathbb{H}
. Then, for all
t\in[\epsilon,T]
and for any
0<\epsilon<T
,

(i)

for
d=2
and
r\in[1,3],
we have

\displaystyle\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

\displaystyle\leq\frac{N_{11}}{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+(N_{12}+t)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(B.11)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{C}{2\mu^{3}}\bigg\{\frac{1}{t^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}+2\bigg(\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}+t^{2}\bigg)\|g\|_{0}^{4}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\bigg\},

where

\displaystyle N_{11}=\frac{3}{2}+\frac{1}{r+1}\ \ \ \text{and}\ \ \ N_{12}=\frac{N_{11}}{\alpha},

(ii)

for
d=2,3
and
r>3,
we have

(B.12)

\displaystyle\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s\leq\bigg(\frac{N_{21}}{t}+N_{22}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(N_{23}t+N_{24}\bigg)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

where

\displaystyle N_{21}=\frac{3}{2}+\frac{1}{r+1}+\frac{1}{2\mu},\ \ \ N_{22}=\frac{\gamma^{*}+\eta}{2\mu},\ \gamma^{*}=\frac{2(r-3)}{r-1}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{4}{\beta(r-1)}}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r-3}},

\displaystyle N_{23}=1+\frac{1}{\mu}+\frac{\gamma^{*}+\eta}{2\mu\alpha}\ \ \ \text{and}\ \ \ N_{24}=\frac{N_{21}}{\alpha},

(iii)

for
d=r=3
with
\beta\mu>1,
we have

(B.13)

\displaystyle\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s\leq\frac{N_{31}}{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(N_{32}t+N_{33}\bigg)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

where

\displaystyle N_{31}=\frac{7}{4}+\frac{1}{2(\beta\mu-1)},\ \ \ N_{32}=1+\frac{1}{\beta\mu-1}\ \ \ \text{and}\ \ \ N_{33}=\frac{N_{31}}{\alpha}.

Proof.

Taking the inner product with
\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\cdot)
in (
1.1
) and integrating the resulting equation over
\mathbb{T}^{d}
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{2}\frac{\/\mathrm{d}\/}{\/\mathrm{d}\/t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{2}\frac{\/\mathrm{d}\/}{\/\mathrm{d}\/t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{r+1}\frac{\/\mathrm{d}\/}{\/\mathrm{d}\/t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}

(B.14)

\displaystyle=(\boldsymbol{f}g(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t))-((\boldsymbol{v}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)),

for a.e.
t\in[\epsilon,T]
, where we have used the fact that
\langle\nabla p,\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\rangle=0
.

Case I:

d=2
and
r\in[1,3]
.

Substituting the estimates (
3.30
) and (
3
) in (
B
) and integrating it from
\epsilon
to
t
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{2\beta}{r+1}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{2\beta}{r+1}\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}+2(t-\epsilon)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(B.15)

\displaystyle\quad+C\sup_{s\in[\epsilon,t]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{4}{3}}}}\right)\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s,

for all
t\in[\epsilon,T]
. Using the estimate (

(
i
)

) in (
B
), we immediately get

\displaystyle\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{2\beta}{r+1}\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}

\displaystyle\quad+2(t-\epsilon)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{C}{2\mu^{3}}\bigg\{\frac{1}{t^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}+2\bigg(\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}+t^{2}\bigg)\|g\|_{0}^{4}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\bigg\}.

Integrating the above estimate over
\epsilon
from
0
to
t
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{t}\bigg[\mu\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/\epsilon+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/\epsilon+\frac{2\beta}{r+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\/\mathrm{d}\/\epsilon

\displaystyle\quad+t^{2}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{Ct}{2\mu^{3}}\bigg\{\frac{1}{t^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}+2\bigg(\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}+t^{2}\bigg)\|g\|_{0}^{4}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}\bigg\}\bigg].

Thus, from (
B
