), we immediately derive (

(
i
)

) by using (
B.1
).

Case II:

d=2,3
and
r>3
.
Using Hölder’s and Young’s inequalities, we have

(B.16)

\displaystyle|(\boldsymbol{f}g,\boldsymbol{v}_{t})|\leq\|g\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq\frac{1}{4}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|g\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

An estimate similar to (
3.17
) yields

(B.17)

\displaystyle|\langle(\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v},\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\rangle|\leq\frac{1}{4}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{2}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\gamma^{*}}{2}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

where
\gamma^{*}=\frac{2(r-3)}{r-1}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{4}{\beta(r-1)}}}\right)^{\frac{2}{r-3}}
. Substituting the estimates (
B.16
) and (
B.17
) in (
B
) and integrating it from
\epsilon
to
t
, we deduce

\displaystyle\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{2\beta}{r+1}\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{2\beta}{r+1}\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}+2(t-\epsilon)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(B.18)

\displaystyle\quad+\beta\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(s)|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\gamma^{*}\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s,

for all
t\in[\epsilon,T]
. We use the energy estimates (
B.1
) and (

(

⁢
i
i

)

) in (
B
) to obtain

\displaystyle\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{2\beta}{r+1}\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}+2(t-\epsilon)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\frac{1}{2\mu t}+\frac{\gamma^{*}+\eta}{2\mu}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(\frac{1}{2\mu\alpha}+\frac{t}{\mu}+\frac{(\gamma^{*}+\eta)t}{2\mu\alpha}\bigg)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Integrating the above estimate over
\epsilon
from
0
to
t
, we get

\displaystyle\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{t}\bigg\{\mu\int_{0}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/\epsilon+\alpha\int_{0}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/\epsilon+\frac{2\beta}{r+1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{r+1}}^{r+1}\/\mathrm{d}\/\epsilon

\displaystyle\quad+t^{2}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+t\bigg(\frac{1}{2\mu t}+\frac{\gamma^{*}+\eta}{2\mu}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+t\bigg(\frac{1}{2\mu\alpha}+\frac{t}{\mu}+\frac{(\gamma^{*}+\eta)t}{2\mu\alpha}\bigg)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\bigg\}.

From (
B
), one can reach (
B.12
) by using the energy estimate (
B.1
).

Case III:

d=r=3
with
\beta\mu>1
.
Using Hölder’s and Young’s inequalities, we have

(B.19)

\displaystyle|\langle(\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v},\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\rangle|\leq\frac{1}{4}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\big\||\boldsymbol{v}||\nabla\boldsymbol{v}|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{2}\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{4}+2(t-\epsilon)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(B.20)

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\epsilon}^{t}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(s)||\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(s)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s,

for all

. Using (

(

⁢
i
i
i

)

) in (

) results in

\displaystyle\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\beta}{2}\|\boldsymbol{v}(\epsilon)\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}^{4}+2(t-\epsilon)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2(\beta\mu-1)}\bigg\{\frac{1}{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(\frac{1}{\alpha}+2t\bigg)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\bigg\}.

Integrating the above estimate over

\displaystyle\quad+t^{2}\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{t}{2(\beta\mu-1)}\bigg\{\frac{1}{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(\frac{1}{\alpha}+2t\bigg)\|g\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\bigg\},

which easily leads to (
B.13
) by (

Lemma B.4
.

Let
(\boldsymbol{v}(\cdot),\nabla p(\cdot))
be the unique solution of the CBF equations (
1.1
)-(
1.4
) and
\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\in\mathbb{H}
. Then, for all
t\in[\epsilon_{1},T]
and for any
0<\epsilon<\epsilon_{1}<T
,

(i)

for
d=2
and
r\in[1,3],
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[\epsilon_{1},t]}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{\epsilon_{1}}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t+\beta\int_{\epsilon_{1}}^{t}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(t)|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

(B.21)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon)^{3}}\bigg)\big(1+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{6}\big)+C\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon)^{3}}\bigg)\bigg(1+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{6}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{6}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{6}\big)\bigg),

(ii)

for
d=2,3
and
r>3,
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[\epsilon_{1},t]}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{\epsilon_{1}}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\beta\int_{\epsilon_{1}}^{t}\big\||\boldsymbol{v}(s)|^{\frac{r-1}{2}}|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)|\big\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

(B.22)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon)^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\bigg(1+\frac{1}{t-\epsilon}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big),

(iii)

(B.23)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{(t-\epsilon)^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\bigg(1+\frac{1}{t-\epsilon}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big).

Proof.

to the equation (

), we find

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}-\mu\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}+(\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{t}+(\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}+\alpha\boldsymbol{v}_{t}+\beta\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v})\boldsymbol{v}_{t}+\nabla p_{t}=\boldsymbol{f}g_{t},

(B.25)

\displaystyle=(\boldsymbol{f}g_{t}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t))-\big((\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\big),

for a.e.

, for some

Lemma B.5
.

Let
\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\in\mathbb{H}
and
\boldsymbol{F}(x,t):=\boldsymbol{f}(x)g(x,t)\in\mathrm{W}^{1,2}(0,T;\mathbb{H})
(which implies
\boldsymbol{F}\in\mathrm{C}([0,T];\mathbb{H})
also). Then, for all
t\in[\epsilon_{1},T]
and for any
0<\epsilon<\epsilon_{1}<T
,

(i)

for
d=2,3
and
r>3,
and for
d=r=3
with
\beta\mu>1
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{1},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\nabla p(t)\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}^{2}}}\right)

(B.29)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon)^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\bigg(1+\frac{1}{t-\epsilon}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big),

(ii)

for
d=2
and
r\in[1,3],
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{1},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\nabla p(t)\|_{\mathbb{L}^{2}}^{2}}}\right)

(B.30)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon)^{3}}\bigg)\big(1+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{6}\big)+C\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon)^{3}}\bigg)\bigg(1+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{6}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{6}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{6}\big)\bigg).

Using the maximal elliptic regularity to the elliptic boundary value problem obtained from (
1.1
), we get the estimate (

(
i
)

) (see Theorem 4.2,
[
27
]
). The estimate for the case of
d=2,\ r\in[1,3]
can be obtained by using the
m
-accretive quantization of the linear and nonlinear operators (cf. Theorems 1.6 and 1.8 in Chapter 4,
[
2
]
for the abstract theory and Section 5,
[
3
]
for 2D NSE).

Lemma B.6
.

Let
(\boldsymbol{v}(\cdot),\nabla p(\cdot))
be the unique solution of the CBF equations (
1.1
)-(
1.4
) and
\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\in\mathbb{H}
. Then, for all
t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]
and for any

,

(i)

for
d=2
and
r>3,
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\int_{\epsilon_{2}}^{T}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

(B.31)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big)\bigg\}^{\frac{r+1}{2}},

(ii)

for
d=2
and
r\in[1,3],
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\int_{\epsilon_{2}}^{T}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

(B.32)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})^{3}}\bigg)\big(1+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{6}\big)+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})^{3}}\bigg)\bigg(1+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{6}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{6}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{6}\big)\bigg)\bigg\}^{\frac{r+1}{2}},

(iii)

for
d=3
and
r\geq 3,
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\int_{\epsilon_{2}}^{T}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

(B.33)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big)\bigg\}^{\frac{3r+1}{4}}.

Proof.

Taking the inner product with
-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\cdot)
in the equation (
B.24
), we find

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\/\mathrm{d}\/}{\/\mathrm{d}\/t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle=(\boldsymbol{f}g_{t}(t),-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t))-((\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}(t),-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t))-((\boldsymbol{v}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t),-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t))

(B.34)

\displaystyle\quad-\beta\big(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v})\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t),-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\big)-(\nabla p_{t},-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t))=:\sum_{i=1}^{5}I_{i},

for a.e.
t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]
, for some
0<\epsilon_{2}\leq T
. Using Hölder’s and Young’s inequalities, we estimate
I_{1}
as

(B.35)

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq|(\boldsymbol{f}g_{t},-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t})|\leq\|g_{t}\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq\frac{7}{2\mu}\|g_{t}\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{14}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Case I:

d=2
and
r>3
.

Using Hölder’s, Gagliardo-Nirenberg’s and Young’s inequalities, we estimates
I_{2}
as

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|((\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v},-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t})|\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu}{14}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}}}\right)

(B.36)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu}{14}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{4}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{3}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Using Hölder’s, Agmon’s and Young’s inequalities, we have

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq|((\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{t},-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t})|

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}}}\right)\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(B.37)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}}}\right)\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{14}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\beta\big|\big(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v})\boldsymbol{v}_{t},-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\big)\big|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{\begin{array}[]{cc}\beta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(|\boldsymbol{v}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{t}+(r-1)\frac{\boldsymbol{v}}{|\boldsymbol{v}|^{3-r}}(\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\boldsymbol{v}_{t}),-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}}}\right)}}\right|,&\text{ for }\boldsymbol{v}\neq\mathbf{0},\\
\mathbf{0},&\text{ for }\boldsymbol{v}=\mathbf{0},\end{array}}}\right.\text{ for }1\leq r<3,\\
\beta\big|\big(|\boldsymbol{v}|^{r-1}\boldsymbol{v}_{t}+(r-1)\boldsymbol{v}|\boldsymbol{v}|^{r-3}(\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\boldsymbol{v}_{t}),-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\big)\big|,\ \ \ \text{ for }r\geq 3\end{array}}}\right.

\displaystyle\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}^{r-1}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{r-1}{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{r-1}{2}}+\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{r-1}{2}}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(B.41)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2(r-1)}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{r-1}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{r-1}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{14}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Taking divergence on both sides of (
B.24
), we get

\displaystyle-\Delta p_{t}

\displaystyle=\nabla\cdot\big[\boldsymbol{f}g_{t}+(\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{t}+(\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}+\beta\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v})\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\big]

(B.42)

\displaystyle p_{t}

\displaystyle=(-\Delta)^{-1}\big[\nabla\cdot\big\{(\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{t}+(\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}+\beta\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v})\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\big\}\big],

in the weak sense and we have used
\nabla\cdot\boldsymbol{f}=0
. Taking gradient on both sides in (
B
) and then using Hölder’s and Young’s inequalities, we estimate the term
I_{5}
as

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq|(\nabla p_{t},-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t})|\leq\|\nabla p_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\|(\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\|(\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}+\beta\|\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v})\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\big)\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\mu}{14}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{4}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{3}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(B.43)

\displaystyle\quad+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}}}\right)\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2(r-1)}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{r-1}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{r-1}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

where we have used the estimates (
B
)-(
B
) in the final inequality.
Substituting the estimates (
B.35
)-(
B
) and (
B
) in (
B
), and then integrating the resulting estimate from
\epsilon_{2}
to
t
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{\epsilon_{2}}^{t}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+2\alpha\int_{\epsilon_{2}}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\epsilon_{2})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C(t-\epsilon_{2})\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C(t-\epsilon_{2})\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}+\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{3}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}}}\right)\int_{\epsilon_{2}}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s

(B.44)

\displaystyle\quad+C(t-\epsilon_{2})\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2(r-1)}+\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{r-1}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{r-1}}}\right)\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

for all
t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]
. Using the energy estimates obtained in Lemmas
B.1
,
B.4
and
B.5
in (
B
), we obtain the following estimate:

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\epsilon_{2})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon)^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{t-\epsilon}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big)\bigg\}^{\frac{r+1}{2}},

for all
t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]
. Integrating the above estimate over
\epsilon_{2}
from
\epsilon_{1}
to
t
and then using the energy estimate obtained in Lemma
B.4
, we deduce

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{t-\epsilon_{1}}\int_{\epsilon_{1}}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\epsilon_{2})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/\epsilon_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon)^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{t-\epsilon}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big)\bigg\}^{\frac{r+1}{2}}

(B.45)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big)\bigg\}^{\frac{r+1}{2}},

for any
0<\epsilon<\epsilon_{1}<\epsilon_{2}<T
. Thus, from (
B
), it is immediate that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\mu\int_{\epsilon_{2}}^{T}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

(B.46)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big)\bigg\}^{\frac{r+1}{2}},

for all
t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]
and
\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\in\mathrm{L}^{2}(\epsilon_{2},T;\mathbb{H})
. One can infer that
\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\in\mathrm{L}^{2}(\epsilon_{2},T;\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}\cap\mathbb{V})
(cf. subsection
2.1
).

Case II:

d=2
and
r\in[1,3]
.

Substituting the estimates (
B.35
)-(
B
) and (
B
) in (
B
), and by using the similar arguments as
d=2
and
r>3
, we obtain the required estimate (

(

⁢
i
i

)

) and
\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\in\mathrm{L}^{2}(\epsilon_{2},T;\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}\cap\mathbb{V})
.

Case III:

d=3
and
r\geq 3
.
Using Hölder’s, Gagliardo-Nirenberg’s and Young’s inequalities, we have

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\leq|((\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v},-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t})|\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{8}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}}^{\frac{7}{8}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu}{14}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{7}{4}}+\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{7}{4}}}}\right)

(B.47)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu}{14}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{4}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{8}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{7}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Making the use of Hölder’s, Agmon’s and Young’s inequalities, we compute

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq|((\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{t},-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t})|

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{4}}}}\right)\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(B.48)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}}}\right)\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{14}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

A calculation similar to (
B
) gives

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\beta\big|\big(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v})\boldsymbol{v}_{t},-\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\big)\big|\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\widetilde{\mathbb{L}}^{\infty}}^{r-1}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{r-1}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}_{p}^{2}}^{\frac{3(r-1)}{4}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{r-1}{4}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3(r-1)}{4}}+\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3(r-1)}{4}}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}

(B.49)

\displaystyle\leq C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2(r-1)}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{r-1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}^{2}}^{\frac{3(r-1)}{2}}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{14}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

An estimate similar to (
B
) yields

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3\mu}{14}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{4}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}^{2}}^{7}+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}}}\right)\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(B.50)

\displaystyle\quad+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2(r-1)}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{r-1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}^{2}}^{\frac{3(r-1)}{2}}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

where we have used the estimates (
B
)-(
B
). Substituting (
B.35
), (
B
)-(
B
) in (
B
), and by using the similar arguments as
d=2
and
r>3
, we obtain (

(

⁢
i
i
i

)

) and
\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\in\mathrm{L}^{2}(\epsilon_{2},T;\mathbb{H}^{2}_{p}\cap\mathbb{V})
.
∎

Lemma B.7
.

Let
(\boldsymbol{v}(\cdot),\nabla p(\cdot))
be the unique solution of the CBF equations (
1.1
)-(
1.4
). Then, for all
t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]
and for any
0<\epsilon<\epsilon_{1}<\epsilon_{2}<T
,

(i)

for
d=2
and
r>3,
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\int_{\epsilon_{2}}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

(B.51)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big)\bigg\}^{\frac{r+1}{2}},

(ii)

for
d=2
and
r\in[1,3],
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\int_{\epsilon_{2}}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

(B.52)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})^{3}}\bigg)\big(1+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{6}\big)+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})^{3}}\bigg)\bigg(1+\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{6}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{6}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{6}\big)\bigg)\bigg\}^{\frac{r+1}{2}}.

(iii)

for
d=3
and
r\geq 3,
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\int_{\epsilon_{2}}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/t

(B.53)

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big)\bigg\}^{\frac{3r+1}{4}}.

Proof.

Taking the inner product with
\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}(\cdot)
in the equation (
B.24
), we deduce

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{2}\frac{\/\mathrm{d}\/}{\/\mathrm{d}\/t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{2}\frac{\/\mathrm{d}\/}{\/\mathrm{d}\/t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle=(\boldsymbol{f}g_{t}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}(t))-((\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}(t))

(B.54)

\displaystyle\quad-((\boldsymbol{v}(t)\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}(t))-\beta(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v})\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t),\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}(t)),

for a.e.
t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]
, for some
0<\epsilon_{2}\leq T
. Using Hölder’s and Young’s inequalities, we estimate
|(\boldsymbol{f}g_{t},\boldsymbol{v}_{tt})|
as

(B.55)

\displaystyle|(\boldsymbol{f}g_{t},\boldsymbol{v}_{tt})|\leq\|g_{t}\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\leq 2\|g_{t}\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Case I:

d=2
and
r>3
.

Calculations similar to (
B
)-(
B
) yield

(B.56)

\displaystyle|((\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v},\boldsymbol{v}_{tt})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}}}\right),

(B.57)

\displaystyle|((\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{t},\boldsymbol{v}_{tt})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}}}\right)\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

(B.58)

\displaystyle|(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v})\boldsymbol{v}_{t},\boldsymbol{v}_{tt})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2(r-1)}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{r-1}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{r-1}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Substituting the estimates (
B.55
)-(
B.58
) in (
B
) and then integrating it from
\epsilon_{2}
to
T
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\int_{\epsilon_{2}}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}(s)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/s+\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\epsilon_{2})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\alpha\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\epsilon_{2})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+4(t-\epsilon_{2})\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C(t-\epsilon_{2})\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\big(\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\big)\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C(t-\epsilon_{2})\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}}}\right)\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

(B.59)

\displaystyle\quad+C(t-\epsilon_{2})\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2(r-1)}+\|\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{r-1}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{r-1}}}\right)\sup_{t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

for all
t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]
. Using the energy estimates obtained in Lemmas
B.1
,
B.4
and
B.5
in (
B
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\epsilon_{2})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{\mu}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\epsilon_{2})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon)^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{t-\epsilon}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big)\bigg\}^{\frac{r+1}{2}},

for all
t\in[\epsilon_{2},T]
. Integrating the above estimate over
\epsilon_{2}
from
\epsilon_{1}
to
t
and then using the energy estimate obtained in Lemma
B.4
, we deduce

\displaystyle\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(t)\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{t-\epsilon_{1}}\bigg(\int_{\epsilon_{1}}^{t}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\epsilon_{2})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/\epsilon_{2}+\frac{\alpha}{\mu}\int_{\epsilon_{1}}^{t}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}(\epsilon_{2})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\/\mathrm{d}\/\epsilon_{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon)^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{t-\epsilon}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big)\bigg\}^{\frac{r+1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg\{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})^{2}}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{0}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(t-\epsilon_{1})}\bigg)\|\boldsymbol{f}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\big(\|g\|_{0}^{2}+\|g_{t}\|_{0}^{2}\big)\bigg\}^{\frac{r+1}{2}},

for any
0<\epsilon<\epsilon_{1}<\epsilon_{2}<T
. Thus, from (
B
), one can reach (

(
i
)

)
and
\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}\in\mathrm{L}^{2}(\epsilon_{2},T;\mathbb{H})
.

Case II:

and

Substituting the estimates (

)-(
B

(B.60)

\displaystyle|((\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v},\boldsymbol{v}_{tt})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{7}{4}}}}\right),

(B.61)

\displaystyle|((\boldsymbol{v}\cdot\nabla)\boldsymbol{v}_{t},\boldsymbol{v}_{tt})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{3}{2}}}}\right)\|\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

(B.62)

\displaystyle\big|\big(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{v})\boldsymbol{v}_{t},\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+C\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2(r-1)}+\|\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{\frac{r-1}{2}}\|\Delta\boldsymbol{v}\|_{\mathbb{H}^{2}}^{\frac{3(r-1)}{2}}}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Substituting (
B.55
) and (
B.60
)-(

) in (

) and then

and

, we obtain the required estimate (

(

⁢
i
i
i

)

) and
\boldsymbol{v}_{tt}\in\mathrm{L}^{2}(\epsilon_{2},T;\mathbb{H})

Remark B.8
.

From Lemmas
B.6
and
B.7
, the facts

\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\in\mathrm{L}^{2}(\epsilon_{2},T;\mathbb{H}^{2}_{p}\cap\mathbb{V})\ \ \text{and}\ \ \boldsymbol{v}_{tt}\in\mathrm{L}^{2}(\epsilon_{2},T;\mathbb{H}),

imply that
\boldsymbol{v}_{t}\in\mathrm{C}([\epsilon_{2},T];\mathbb{V})
for any
0<\epsilon<\epsilon_{1}<\epsilon_{2}\leq T
.
